```
   209→
   210→def _cmd_cluster_reorder(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    raw_names: str = getattr(args, "cluster_names", "") or getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   212→    cluster_names: list[str] = [n.strip() for n in raw_names.split(",") if n.strip()]
   213→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
   214→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
   215→    item_pattern: str | None = getattr(args, "item_pattern", None)
   216→
   217→    plan = load_plan()
   218→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   219→
   220→    # Validate all names exist
   221→    for name in cluster_names:
   222→        if name not in clusters:
   223→            print(colorize(f"  Cluster {name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   224→            return
   225→
   226→    if item_pattern is not None:
   227→        # Within-cluster item reorder
   228→        if len(cluster_names) != 1:
   229→            print(colorize("  --item requires exactly one cluster name.", "red"))
   230→            return
   231→        cluster_name = cluster_names[0]
   232→        cluster_member_set = set(clusters[cluster_name].get("issue_ids", []))
   233→
   234→        state = command_runtime(args).state
   235→        item_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, [item_pattern], plan=plan)
   236→        if not item_ids:
   237→            print(colorize("  No matching issues found for --item pattern.", "yellow"))
   238→            return
   239→        for fid in item_ids:
   240→            if fid not in cluster_member_set:
   241→                print(colorize(f"  {fid!r} is not a member of cluster {cluster_name!r}.", "red"))
   242→                return
   243→
   244→        queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   245→        ordered_slice = [fid for fid in queue_order if fid in cluster_member_set]
   246→
   247→        offset: int | None = None
   248→        resolved_target: str | None = None
   249→        item_set = set(item_ids)
   250→
   251→        if position == "top":
   252→            first_non_item = next((fid for fid in ordered_slice if fid not in item_set), None)
   253→            if first_non_item is None or set(ordered_slice[:len(item_ids)]) == item_set:
   254→                print(colorize("  Already at the top of the cluster.", "yellow"))
   255→                return
   256→            resolved_target = first_non_item
   257→            position = "before"
   258→        elif position == "bottom":
   259→            last_non_item = next((fid for fid in reversed(ordered_slice) if fid not in item_set), None)
   260→            if last_non_item is None or set(ordered_slice[-len(item_ids):]) == item_set:
   261→                print(colorize("  Already at the bottom of the cluster.", "yellow"))
   262→                return
   263→            resolved_target = last_non_item
   264→            position = "after"
   265→        elif position in ("before", "after"):
   266→            if target is None:
   267→                print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires a target. Example: --item PAT {position} TARGET", "red"))
   268→                return
   269→            target_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, [target], plan=plan)
   270→            if not target_ids:
   271→                print(colorize(f"  No match for target {target!r}.", "yellow"))
   272→                return
   273→            resolved_target = target_ids[0]
   274→            if resolved_target not in cluster_member_set:
   275→                print(colorize(f"  Target {resolved_target!r} is not in cluster {cluster_name!r}.", "red"))
   276→                return
   277→        elif position in ("up", "down"):
   278→            if target is None:
   279→                print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires an offset. Example: --item PAT {position} 3", "red"))
   280→                return
   281→            try:
   282→                offset = int(target)
   283→            except (ValueError, TypeError):
   284→                print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   285→                return
   286→
   287→        count = move_items(plan, item_ids, position, target=resolved_target, offset=offset)
   288→        append_log_entry(
   289→            plan, "cluster_reorder", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   290→            detail={"position": position, "count": count, "item": item_pattern},
   291→        )
   292→        save_plan(plan)
   293→        print(colorize(f"  Moved {count} item(s) to {position} within cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   294→        return
   295→
   296→    # Whole-cluster block reorder (original behaviour)
   297→    target = resolve_target(plan, target, position)
   298→
   299→    offset = None
   300→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   301→        try:
   302→            offset = int(target)
   303→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   304→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   305→            return
   306→        target = None
   307→
   308→    seen: set[str] = set()
   309→    all_member_ids: list[str] = []
   310→    for name in cluster_names:
   311→        for fid in clusters[name].get("issue_ids", []):
   312→            if fid not in seen:
   313→                seen.add(fid)
   314→                all_member_ids.append(fid)
   315→
   316→    if not all_member_ids:
   317→        print(colorize("  No members in the specified cluster(s).", "yellow"))
   318→        return
   319→
   320→    count = move_items(plan, all_member_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   321→    append_log_entry(
   322→        plan, "cluster_reorder", cluster_name=",".join(cluster_names), actor="user",
   323→        detail={"position": position, "count": count},
   324→    )
   325→    save_plan(plan)
   326→    label = ", ".join(cluster_names)
   327→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster(s) {label} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   328→
   329→
   330→def _print_cluster_member(idx: int, fid: str, issue: dict | None) -> None:
   331→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional issue details."""
   332→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   333→    if not issue:
   334→        return
   335→    file = issue.get("file", "")
   336→    lines = issue.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
   337→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   338→    if file:
   339→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   340→    summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   341→    if summary:
   342→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def _load_issues_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   346→    """Load issues from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   347→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   348→    return rt.state.get("issues", {})
   349→
   350→
   351→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   352→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   353→    plan = load_plan()
   354→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   355→    if cluster is None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Header
   360→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   361→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   362→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   363→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   364→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   365→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   366→    if desc:
   367→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   368→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   369→    if action:
   370→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   371→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   372→    if steps:
   373→        print(colorize(f"  Steps ({len(steps)}):", "dim"))
   374→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   375→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {step}", "dim"))
   376→
   377→    # Members
   378→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   379→    if not issue_ids:
   380→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   381→    else:
   382→        issues = _load_issues_best_effort(args)
   383→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(issue_ids)}):", "dim"))
   384→        for idx, fid in enumerate(issue_ids, 1):
   385→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, issues.get(fid))
   386→
   387→    # Commands
   388→    print()
   389→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   390→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   391→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   392→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   393→
   394→
   395→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   396→    plan = load_plan()
   397→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   398→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   399→    verbose: bool = getattr(args, "verbose", False)
   400→
   401→    if not clusters:
   402→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   403→        return
   404→
   405→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   406→    pos_map = {fid: i for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order)}
   407→
   408→    def _min_pos(cluster_data: dict) -> int:
   409→        positions = [pos_map[fid] for fid in cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []) if fid in pos_map]
   410→        return min(positions) if positions else 999_999
   411→
   412→    min_pos_cache = {name: _min_pos(c) for name, c in clusters.items()}
   413→    sorted_clusters = sorted(clusters.items(), key=lambda kv: min_pos_cache[kv[0]])
   414→
   415→    if verbose:
   416→        name_width = max(20, min(35, max(len(n) for n, _ in sorted_clusters)))
   417→        total = len(sorted_clusters)
   418→        print(colorize(f"  Clusters ({total} total, sorted by queue position):", "bold"))
   419→        print()
   420→        header = f"  {'#pos':<5}  {'Name':<{name_width}}  {'Items':>5}  {'Steps':>5}  {'Type':<6}  Description"
   421→        sep = f"  {'─'*4}  {'─'*name_width}  {'─'*5}  {'─'*5}  {'─'*6}  {'─'*45}"
   422→        print(colorize(header, "dim"))
   423→        print(colorize(sep, "dim"))
   424→        for name, cluster in sorted_clusters:
   425→            min_p = min_pos_cache[name]
   426→            pos_str = f"#{min_p}" if min_p < 999_999 else "—"
   427→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   428→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   429→            steps_str = str(len(steps)) if steps else "—"
   430→            type_str = "auto" if cluster.get("auto") else "manual"
   431→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   432→            if not desc and min_p == 999_999 and not member_count:
   433→                desc = "(no queue position — no members)"
   434→            desc_truncated = (desc[:44] + "…") if len(desc) > 45 else desc
   435→            name_display = (name[:name_width - 1] + "…") if len(name) > name_width else name
   436→            focused = " *" if name == active else ""
   437→            print(f"  {pos_str:>5}  {name_display:<{name_width}}  {member_count:>5}  {steps_str:>5}  {type_str:<6}  {desc_truncated}{focused}")
   438→        print()
   439→        return
   440→
   441→    print(colorize("  Clusters (ordered by queue position):", "bold"))
   442→    for name, cluster in sorted_clusters:
   443→        min_p = min_pos_cache[name]
   444→        pos_str = f"#{min_p}" if min_p < 999_999 else "—"
   445→        member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   446→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   447→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   448→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   449→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   450→        print(f"    {pos_str:>5}  {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   451→
   452→
   453→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   454→    """Update cluster description and/or action_steps."""
   455→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   456→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
   457→    steps: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "steps", None)
   458→
   459→    if description is None and steps is None:
   460→        print(colorize("  Nothing to update. Use --description and/or --steps.", "yellow"))
   461→        return
   462→
   463→    plan = load_plan()
   464→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   465→    if cluster is None:
   466→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   467→        return
   468→
   469→    if description is not None:
   470→        cluster["description"] = description
   471→    if steps is not None:
   472→        cluster["action_steps"] = list(steps)
   473→        print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
   474→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   475→            clean = _LEADING_NUM_RE.sub('', step)
   476→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {clean}", "dim"))
   477→        if len(steps) == 0:
   478→            print(colorize("  Warning: 0 steps stored. Did you forget the --steps values?", "yellow"))
   479→        elif len(steps) == 1 and len(steps[0]) > 100:
   480→            print(colorize("  Warning: only 1 step stored and it's quite long. Check shell quoting.", "yellow"))
   481→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   482→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   483→    append_log_entry(
   484→        plan, "cluster_update", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   485→        detail={"description": description, "steps": steps},
   486→    )
   487→    save_plan(plan)
   488→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   489→
   490→
   491→def _cmd_cluster_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   492→    """Merge source cluster into target cluster."""
   493→    source: str = getattr(args, "source", "")
   494→    target: str = getattr(args, "target", "")
   495→
   496→    plan = load_plan()
   497→    try:
   498→        added, source_ids = merge_clusters(plan, source, target)
   499→    except ValueError as ex:
   500→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   501→        return
   502→
   503→    append_log_entry(
   504→        plan, "cluster_merge", issue_ids=source_ids,
   505→        cluster_name=target, actor="user",
   506→        detail={"source": source, "added": added},
   507→    )
   508→    save_plan(plan)
   509→    print(colorize(
   510→        f"  Merged cluster {source!r} into {target!r}: "
   511→        f"{added} issue(s) added, {len(source_ids)} total moved. Source deleted.",
   512→        "green",
   513→    ))
   514→
   515→
   516→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   517→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   518→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   519→    dispatch = {
   520→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   521→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   522→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   523→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   524→        "reorder": _cmd_cluster_reorder,
   525→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   526→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   527→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   528→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   529→    }
   530→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   531→    if handler is None:
   532→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   533→        return
   534→    handler(args)
   535→
   536→
   537→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   538→
```

```
1→"""Metadata and logging mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    ensure_plan_defaults,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    11→
    12→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    13→
    14→
    15→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    16→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    17→    try:
    18→        from desloppify.base.config import load_config
    19→
    20→        config = load_config()
    21→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    22→        return max(0, int(value))
    23→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    24→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    25→
    26→
    27→def append_log_entry(
    28→    plan: PlanModel,
    29→    action: str,
    30→    *,
    31→    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    32→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    33→    actor: str = "user",
    34→    note: str | None = None,
    35→    detail: dict | None = None,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    38→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    39→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    40→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    41→        "action": action,
    42→        "issue_ids": issue_ids or [],
    43→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    44→        "actor": actor,
    45→        "note": note,
    46→        "detail": detail or {},
    47→    }
    48→    log.append(entry)
    49→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    50→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    51→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    52→
    53→
    54→def describe_issue(
    55→    plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str, description: str | None
    56→) -> None:
    57→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a issue."""
    58→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    59→    now = utc_now()
    60→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
    61→    if issue_id not in overrides:
    62→        overrides[issue_id] = {"issue_id": issue_id, "created_at": now}
    63→    overrides[issue_id]["description"] = description
    64→    overrides[issue_id]["updated_at"] = now
    65→
    66→
    67→def annotate_issue(
    68→    plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str, note: str | None
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Set or clear a note on a issue."""
    71→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    72→    now = utc_now()
    73→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
    74→    if issue_id not in overrides:
    75→        overrides[issue_id] = {"issue_id": issue_id, "created_at": now}
    76→    overrides[issue_id]["note"] = note
    77→    overrides[issue_id]["updated_at"] = now
    78→
    79→
    80→__all__ = ["annotate_issue", "append_log_entry", "describe_issue"]
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_meta import append_log_entry
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_skip import resurface_stale_skips
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    SupersededEntry,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    19→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    20→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    21→    sync_triage_needed,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass
    29→class ReconcileResult:
    30→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    31→
    32→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    changes: int = 0
    36→
    37→
    38→def _find_candidates(
    39→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    40→) -> list[str]:
    41→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    42→    candidates: list[str] = []
    43→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    44→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    45→            continue
    46→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    47→            candidates.append(fid)
    48→    return candidates
    49→
    50→
    51→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    53→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    54→    if issue is None:
    55→        return False
    56→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    57→
    58→
    59→def _supersede_id(
    60→    plan: PlanModel,
    61→    state: StateModel,
    62→    issue_id: str,
    63→    now: str,
    64→) -> bool:
    65→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    66→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    67→    detector = ""
    68→    file = ""
    69→    summary = ""
    70→    if issue:
    71→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    72→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    73→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    74→
    75→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    76→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    77→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    78→
    79→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    80→        "original_id": issue_id,
    81→        "original_detector": detector,
    82→        "original_file": file,
    83→        "original_summary": summary,
    84→        "status": "superseded",
    85→        "superseded_at": now,
    86→        "remapped_to": None,
    87→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    # Preserve any existing override note
    91→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    92→    if override_note:
    93→        entry["note"] = override_note
    94→
    95→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    96→
    97→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
    98→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    99→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   100→    if issue_id in order:
   101→        order.remove(issue_id)
   102→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   103→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   104→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   105→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   106→        if issue_id in ids:
   107→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   108→
   109→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   110→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   111→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   112→        override["cluster"] = None
   113→        override["updated_at"] = now
   114→
   115→    return True
   116→
   117→
   118→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   119→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   120→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   121→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   122→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   123→
   124→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   125→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   126→        try:
   127→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   128→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   129→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   130→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   131→                to_prune.append(fid)
   132→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   133→            to_prune.append(fid)
   134→
   135→    for fid in to_prune:
   136→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   137→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   138→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   139→
   140→    return to_prune
   141→
   142→
   143→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   144→    plan: PlanModel,
   145→    state: StateModel,
   146→) -> ReconcileResult:
   147→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   148→
   149→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   150→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   151→    """
   152→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   153→    result = ReconcileResult()
   154→    now = utc_now()
   155→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   156→
   157→    # Collect all issue IDs referenced by the plan
   158→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   159→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   160→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   161→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   162→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   163→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   164→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   165→
   166→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   167→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   168→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   169→    referenced_ids = {
   170→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   171→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    # Check each referenced ID
   175→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   176→        if not _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   177→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   178→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   179→                result.changes += 1
   180→
   181→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead issues, delete empty epics
   182→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   183→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   184→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   185→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   186→            continue
   187→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   188→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   189→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   190→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   191→            result.changes += 1
   192→        if not alive_ids:
   193→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   194→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   195→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   196→        result.changes += 1
   197→
   198→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   199→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   200→
```

```
    70→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    71→    if not path.exists():
    72→        return None
    73→    return path.read_text()
    74→
    75→
    76→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
```

```
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:

    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).

    76→
    77→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    78→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    79→
    80→
    81→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    82→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    83→
    84→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    85→    total: int
    86→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    87→    new_ids: set[str]
    88→
    89→
    90→def build_work_queue(
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    94→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    95→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
    96→
    97→    Pipeline:
    98→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    99→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
   100→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   101→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   102→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   103→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   104→    """
   105→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   106→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   107→
   108→    # 1. Gather
   109→    items = build_issue_items(
   110→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   111→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   112→    )
   113→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   114→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   115→
   116→    # 2. Score & filter
   117→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   118→    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]
   119→
   120→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   121→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   122→
   123→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   124→    items = _apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   125→
   126→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   127→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   128→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   129→
   130→    # 6. Finalize
   131→    if not items:
   132→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   133→    total = len(items)
   134→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   135→        items = items[:opts.count]
   136→    if opts.explain:
   137→        for item in items:
   138→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   139→
   140→    return {
   141→        "items": items,
   142→        "total": total,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   144→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   145→    }
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→
   153→def _resolve_inputs(
   154→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   155→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   156→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   157→    ctx = opts.context
   158→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   159→
   160→    scan_path: str | None = (
   161→        state.get("scan_path")
   162→        if opts.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   163→        else opts.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   164→    )
   165→
   166→    status = opts.status
   167→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   168→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   169→
   170→    try:
   171→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   172→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   173→        threshold = 100.0
   174→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   175→
   176→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   177→
   178→
   179→def _gather_subjective_items(
   180→    state: StateModel,
   181→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   182→    threshold: float,
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   185→
   186→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in _apply_lifecycle_filter,
   187→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   188→    """
   189→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   190→        return []
   191→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   192→        return []
   193→    if opts.chronic:
   194→        return []
   195→
   196→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   197→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   198→    )
   199→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   200→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any

     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import (

    16→
    17→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    18→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    19→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not scan_history:
    21→        return set()
    22→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    23→    if not threshold:
    24→        return set()
    25→    return {
    26→        issue_id
    27→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    28→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    29→    }
    30→
    31→
    32→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    33→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    34→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    35→
    36→    for item in items:
    37→        issue_id = item["id"]
    38→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    39→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    40→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    41→        if description:
    42→            item["plan_description"] = description
    43→        if note:
    44→            item["plan_note"] = note
    45→        if override.get("cluster"):
    46→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    47→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    48→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    49→                "name": cluster_name,
    50→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    51→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", [])),
    52→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    53→            }
    54→
    55→
    56→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    new_ids: set[str],
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    62→
    63→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    64→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    65→    """
    66→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    67→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    68→
    69→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    70→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    71→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    72→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    73→
    74→    for item in items:
    75→        item_id = item["id"]
    76→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    77→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    78→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    79→
    80→
    81→def separate_skipped(
    82→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    83→    plan: dict,
    84→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    85→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    88→    """
    89→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    90→    if not skipped_ids:
    91→        return items, []
    92→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    93→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    96→            skipped.append(item)
    97→        else:
    98→            non_skipped.append(item)
    99→    return non_skipped, skipped
   100→
   101→
   102→def filter_cluster_focus(
   103→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    cluster: str | None,
   106→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   109→    if not effective_cluster:
   110→        return items
   111→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   112→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   113→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   115→        return items
   116→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   117→
   118→
   119→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   120→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   121→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   122→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   123→
   124→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→
   136→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   137→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   138→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   139→    if meta:
   140→        return meta.action_type
   141→    return "manual_fix"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _build_cluster_meta(
   145→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   146→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   147→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   148→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   149→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   150→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   151→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   152→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   153→        action_type = "reorganize"
   154→    else:
   155→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   156→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   157→            action_type = "refactor"
   158→
   159→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   160→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   161→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   162→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   163→    else:
   164→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   165→
   166→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   167→    if not primary_command:
   168→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   169→
   170→    estimated_impact = max(
   171→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   172→    )
   173→
   174→    return {
   175→        "id": cluster_name,
   176→        "kind": "cluster",
   177→        "action_type": action_type,
   178→        "summary": summary,
   179→        "members": members,
   180→        "member_count": len(members),
   181→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   182→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   183→        "cluster_auto": True,
   184→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   185→        "confidence": "high",
   186→        "detector": detector,
   187→        "file": "",
   188→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   189→    }
   190→
   191→
   192→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   193→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   194→
   195→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   196→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   197→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   198→    """
   199→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   200→    if not clusters:
   201→        return items
   202→
   203→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   204→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   205→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   206→            continue
   207→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   208→            fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   209→
   210→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   211→        return items
   212→
   213→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   214→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   215→    for item in items:
   216→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   217→        if cname:
   218→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   219→
   220→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   221→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   222→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   223→        if len(members) < 2:
   224→            continue
   225→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   226→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   230→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   231→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   232→    result: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   233→    for item in items:
   234→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   235→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   236→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   237→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   238→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   239→            # skip individual member
   240→        else:
   241→            result.append(item)
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→
   245→__all__ = [
   246→    "collapse_clusters",
   247→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   248→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   249→    "new_item_ids",
   250→    "separate_skipped",
   251→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   252→    "stamp_positions",
   253→]
```

```
   202→def _gather_workflow_items(
   203→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   204→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   205→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   206→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   210→    for builder in (
   211→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   212→        build_import_scores_item,
   213→        build_communicate_score_item,
   214→    ):
   215→        item = builder(plan, state)
   216→        if item is not None:
   217→            items.append(item)
   218→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   219→    if plan_item is not None:
   220→        items.append(plan_item)
   221→    return items
   222→
   223→
   224→_MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   225→
   226→
   227→def _passes_impact_floor(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   228→    """Return True if item should survive the impact floor filter."""
   229→    if item.get("kind") != "issue":
   230→        return True
   231→    if item.get("is_review") or item.get("is_subjective"):
   232→        return True
   233→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact")
   234→    return not impact or impact >= _MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   235→
   236→
   237→def _plan_presort(
   238→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   239→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   240→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   241→
   242→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   243→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   244→    """
   245→    if not plan:
   246→        return set(), []
   247→
   248→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   249→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   250→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   251→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   252→    items[:] = remaining
   253→    return new_ids, skipped
   254→
   255→
   256→def _plan_postsort(
   257→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   258→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   259→    plan: dict | None,
   260→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   261→) -> None:
   262→    """Re-append skipped items, stamp positions, filter to cluster focus."""
   263→    if not plan:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    if opts.include_skipped:
   267→        items.extend(skipped)
   268→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   269→    focused = filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, opts.cluster)
   270→    items[:] = focused
   271→
   272→
   273→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
   274→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue."""
   275→    return any(
   276→        i.get("kind") == "issue"
   277→        and i.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   278→        for i in items
   279→    )
   280→
   281→
   282→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
   283→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
   284→    return (
   285→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
   286→        and not item.get("initial_review")
   287→    )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   291→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules.
   292→
   293→    Endgame-only items (subjective reassessment) are filtered out when
   294→    objective mechanical work remains. Initial review items always survive
   295→    — they're onboarding priority, not endgame work.
   296→    """
   297→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
   298→        return items  # endgame: everything visible
   299→    return [i for i in items if not _is_endgame_only(i)]
   300→
   301→
   302→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   303→    """Return a 'run scan' nudge when an active plan cycle has cleared."""
   304→    if not plan:
   305→        return []
   306→    plan_scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   307→    if plan_scores.get("strict") is None:
   308→        return []
   309→    return [{
   310→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   311→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   312→        "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   313→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   314→        "file": "",
   315→        "detector": "workflow",
   316→        "confidence": "high",
   317→    }]
   318→

   324→    "WorkQueueResult",
   325→    "build_work_queue",
   326→    "collapse_clusters",
   327→    "group_queue_items",
   328→]
```

```
   187→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   188→    *,
   189→    kind: str,
   190→    state_file: Path | None,
   191→    issue_ids: list[str],
   192→    note: str | None,

   194→) -> dict | None:

   210→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   211→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   212→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   213→    state = runtime.state
   214→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   215→        return
   216→
   217→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   218→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   219→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   220→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   221→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   222→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   223→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   224→
   225→    kind = skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
   226→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(
   227→        kind=kind,
   228→        attestation=attestation,
   229→        note=note,
   230→    ):
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   234→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   235→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   236→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   237→    if not issue_ids:
   238→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   242→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   243→        kind=kind,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   246→        note=note,
   247→        attestation=attestation,
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   251→    count = skip_items(
   252→        plan,
   253→        issue_ids,
   254→        kind=kind,
   255→        reason=reason,
   256→        note=note,
   257→        attestation=attestation,
   258→        review_after=review_after,
   259→        scan_count=scan_count,
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Log the skip action
   263→    append_log_entry(
   264→        plan,
   265→        "skip",
   266→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   267→        actor="user",
   268→        note=note,
   269→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   270→    )
   271→    if state_data is not None:
   272→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   273→            plan=plan,
   274→            plan_path=plan_file,
   275→            state_data=state_data,
   276→            state_path_value=state_file,
   277→        )
   278→    else:
   279→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   280→
   281→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   282→    if review_after:
   283→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   284→    print_user_message(
   285→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   286→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   287→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   288→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   289→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   290→    )
   291→
   292→
   293→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   294→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   295→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   296→    state = runtime.state
   297→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   298→        return
   299→
```

```
   340→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   341→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   342→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   343→
   344→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   345→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   346→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   347→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   348→
   349→    reopened: list[str] = []
   350→    for pattern in patterns:
   351→        reopened.extend(
   352→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   353→        )
   354→
   355→    if not reopened:
   356→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   360→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   361→
   362→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   363→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   364→
   365→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   366→    count = 0
   367→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   368→    for fid in reopened:
   369→        if fid in skipped:
   370→            skipped.pop(fid)
   371→            count += 1
   372→        if fid not in order:
   373→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   374→            order.add(fid)
   375→            count += 1
   376→    append_log_entry(
   377→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   378→    )
   379→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   380→        plan=plan,
   381→        plan_path=plan_file,
   382→        state_data=state_data,
   383→        state_path_value=state_file,
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   387→    if count:

   391→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   392→
   393→
   394→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   395→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   396→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   397→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   398→    for pattern in patterns:
   399→        if pattern in clusters:
   400→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   401→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   402→            ids = [
   403→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   404→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   405→            ]
   406→            if len(ids) == 0:
   407→                print(colorize(
   408→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   409→                    "yellow",
   410→                ))
   411→                print(colorize(
   412→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   413→                    "dim",
   414→                ))
   415→                return True  # blocked
   416→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   417→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   418→                return True  # blocked
   419→    return False  # OK
   420→
   421→
   422→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   423→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   424→    print(colorize(
   425→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   426→        "yellow",
   427→    ))
   428→    for fid in issue_ids:
   429→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   430→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   431→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   432→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   433→    print(colorize(
   434→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   435→        "dim",
   436→    ))
   437→    print(colorize(
   438→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   439→        "dim",
   440→    ))
   441→
   442→
   443→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   444→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   445→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   446→
   447→
   448→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   449→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   450→
   451→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   452→    """
   453→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   454→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   455→    return synthetic, remaining
   456→
   457→
   458→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   459→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   460→
   461→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   462→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   463→    """
   464→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   465→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   466→    if not present:
   467→        return {}
   468→
   469→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   470→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   471→
   472→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   473→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   474→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   475→            continue
   476→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   477→        unmet = sorted(
   478→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   479→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   480→        )
   481→        if unmet:
   482→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   483→    return blocked
   484→
   485→
   486→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   487→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   488→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   489→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   490→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   491→
   492→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   493→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   494→        if not note:
   495→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   496→            return
   497→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   498→        args.attest = attestation
   499→
```

```
     2→

    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# Helpers

    15→def _plan_with_cluster(name: str, issue_ids: list[str]) -> dict:
    16→    plan = empty_plan()
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    plan["clusters"][name] = {
    19→        "name": name,
    20→        "issue_ids": issue_ids,
    21→        "auto": True,
    22→        "cluster_key": f"auto::{name}",
    23→        "action": "autofix",
    24→        "user_modified": False,
    25→    }

    27→

    29→def _state_with_issues(*ids: str) -> dict:
    30→    issues = {}
    31→    for fid in ids:
    32→        issues[fid] = {
    33→            "id": fid,
    34→            "status": "open",
    35→            "detector": "test",
    36→            "file": "test.py",
    37→            "tier": 1,
    38→            "confidence": "high",
    39→            "summary": f"Issue {fid}",
    40→        }

    48→def test_cluster_guard_blocks_small_cluster():
    49→    """Clusters with <= threshold items should be blocked."""
    50→    ids = [f"f{i}" for i in range(5)]
    51→    plan = _plan_with_cluster("auto/test", ids)
    52→    state = _state_with_issues(*ids)
    53→
    54→    blocked = _check_cluster_guard(["auto/test"], plan, state)
    55→    assert blocked is True
    56→

    59→    """Empty clusters should be blocked — must add items first."""

    61→    state = _state_with_issues()
    62→
    63→    blocked = _check_cluster_guard(["auto/test"], plan, state)

    68→    assert "add items" in captured.out.lower()
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups issues into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import AUTO_PREFIX
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster_sync import (
     8→    prune_stale_clusters as _prune_stale_clusters,
     9→    sync_issue_clusters as _sync_issue_clusters,
    10→    sync_subjective_clusters as _sync_subjective_clusters,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import SubjectiveVisibility
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Repair
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
    21→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
    22→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    23→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    24→    repaired = 0
    25→    for override in overrides.values():
    26→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
    27→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
    28→            override["cluster"] = None
    29→            override["updated_at"] = now
    30→            repaired += 1
    31→    return repaired
    32→
    33→
    34→def auto_cluster_issues(
    35→    plan: PlanModel,
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    *,
    38→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    39→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
    40→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    41→) -> int:
    42→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open issues.
    43→
    44→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
    45→    """
    46→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    47→
    48→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    49→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→
    51→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
    52→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
    53→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
    54→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    55→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
    56→            if ck:
    57→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
    58→
    59→    now = utc_now()
    60→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
    61→    changes = 0
    62→
    63→    changes += _sync_issue_clusters(
    64→        plan, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
    65→    )
    66→    changes += _sync_subjective_clusters(
    67→        plan, state, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
    68→        target_strict=target_strict,
    69→        policy=policy,
    70→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
    71→    )
    72→    changes += _prune_stale_clusters(
    73→        plan, issues, clusters, active_auto_keys, now,
    74→    )
    75→    changes += _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
    76→
    77→    plan["updated"] = now
    78→    return changes
    79→
    80→
    81→__all__ = [
    82→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
    83→    "auto_cluster_issues",
    84→]
    85→
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me provide a comprehensive summary:

---

## Desloppify Plan System: Clusters and Task Resolution

Based on my thorough analysis of the codebase, here's how the desloppify plan system handles clusters and task resolution:

### 1. **Cluster Structure** (from `schema.py`)

Clusters are TypedDict objects with these fields:

```python
class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    name: Required[str]
    description: str | None
    issue_ids: list[str]           # Array of issue IDs in this cluster
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    auto: bool                     # True for auto-generated clusters
    cluster_key: str               # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    action: str | None             # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    user_modified: bool            # True when user manually edits membership
    optional: bool
    thesis: str
    direction: str
    root_cause: str
    supersedes: list[str]
    dismissed: list[str]
    agent_safe: bool
    dependency_order: int
    action_steps: list[str]        # Step-by-step guide to resolve the cluster
    source_clusters: list[str]
    status: str
    triage_version: int
```

Issues map to clusters through `plan["overrides"][issue_id]["cluster"] = cluster_name`.

### 2. **How `resolve` Works with Clusters**

**In `app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`:**
- When you resolve issues via `desloppify resolve`, the system calls `_update_living_plan_after_resolve()` which:
  - Calls `purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)` to remove resolved issues from the plan
  - `purge_ids` (in `operations_lifecycle.py`) removes IDs from:
    - `queue_order` list
    - `skipped` dict
    - All cluster `issue_ids` lists
    - Clears cluster references in `overrides`

**In `app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` (for `desloppify plan resolve`):**
- There's a **cluster completion guard** (`_check_cluster_guard`) that BLOCKS resolving small clusters in bulk:
  - If a pattern matches a cluster name and has <= 10 items (threshold), it shows a warning listing all items
  - User must resolve each item individually first
  - Empty clusters are also blocked (must add items first)
  - Clusters with > 10 items can be resolved in one batch
  - Code at lines 394-441:
    ```python
    def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
        """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
        for pattern in patterns:
            if pattern in clusters:
                cluster = clusters[pattern]
                # Filter to alive issues only
                ids = [fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
                       if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"]
                if len(ids) == 0:
                    # Empty cluster — blocked
                    return True
                if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:  # 10 items
                    # Small cluster — blocked, show items
                    _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
                    return True
        return False
    ```

### 3. **Is There Cluster Completion Detection?**

**NO automatic "cluster done" logic exists yet.** Here's what the code does when all cluster items are resolved:

- When the last issue in a cluster is resolved via `resolve` or `plan resolve`, it gets purged from the cluster's `issue_ids` list
- The cluster itself is NOT automatically deleted or marked as "done"
- The cluster remains in `plan["clusters"]` with an empty `issue_ids` list
- An execution log entry is created with `action="done"` (or `action="resolve"`) and `cluster_name` populated if the resolved item was in a cluster
- See `operations_meta.py` line 39 for ExecutionLogEntry schema — the log CAN track `cluster_name` but there is NO `action="cluster_done"` event

Looking at the schema (line 77 in `schema.py`):
```python
action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
```

**There IS a `"cluster_done"` action type defined**, but searching the entire codebase shows it's **never actually appended**. It exists as a placeholder but has no implementation.

### 4. **How `desloppify next` Picks Tasks**

From `app/commands/next/cmd.py` and `engine/_work_queue/`:

- Builds a unified work queue from issues and synthetic items
- Plan integration happens in `_plan_postsort()` (core.py line 256):
  - Stamps cluster membership on items via `enrich_plan_metadata()` (plan_order.py line 32)
  - Filters items if `active_cluster` is set via `filter_cluster_focus()` (plan_order.py line 102)
- If an `active_cluster` is set in the plan, `next` shows only items from that cluster
- Can explicitly request cluster focus: `desloppify next --cluster my-cluster --count 10`
- From `render.py` lines 340-346:
  ```python
  if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
      cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
      clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
      cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
      total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
      print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
  ```

### 5. **Queue Structure**

The queue is stored in `plan["queue_order"]` as a simple list of issue IDs:
```python
queue_order: list[str]  # Global priority-ordered list
```

Issues belong to clusters through the override mechanism:
```python
overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]  # issue_id → {cluster: cluster_name, description, note, ...}
```

Clusters themselves are in a separate dict:
```python
clusters: dict[str, Cluster]  # cluster_name → Cluster object with issue_ids list
```

### 6. **Plan Focus Feature** (`desloppify plan focus`)

From `override_handlers.py` lines 586-622:
- `plan["active_cluster"]` stores the currently focused cluster name
- When set, `desloppify next` automatically scopes to that cluster
- `set_focus(plan, cluster_name)` and `clear_focus(plan)` manage this
- Logs a "focus" action entry in execution_log with `detail={"action": "set" or "clear"}`

### 7. **Reconciliation After Scan** (`operations_lifecycle.py`, `reconcile.py`)

When issues disappear after a scan (lines 181-196 in `reconcile.py`):
- Epic clusters (name starts with "epic/") have dead issues removed
- If an epic cluster becomes empty, it's deleted automatically
- Regular user clusters keep their structure even if members disappear
- Dead members stay in `oversedes` tracking

### 8. **Key Missing: Cluster Completion Signal**

Currently there is **NO mechanism to detect "all cluster members are resolved"** and:
- No automatic cluster cleanup when empty
- No special event/notification when a cluster is cleared
- No workflow milestone for completing a cluster
- The `cluster_done` action type exists in the schema but is unused

---

## Summary Table

| Aspect | Implementation |
|--------|-----------------|
| **Cluster structure** | TypedDict with `name`, `issue_ids[]`, `action_steps[]`, metadata |
| **Issue-to-cluster mapping** | Via `overrides[issue_id]["cluster"] = cluster_name` |
| **Resolve with cluster members** | `purge_ids()` removes from all cluster lists + clears override ref |
| **Bulk resolve guard** | Blocks resolving clusters with ≤10 items (must do individually) |
| **"All cluster resolved" detection** | NO - cluster remains with empty `issue_ids[]` |
| **Cluster cleanup** | Only epic/* clusters auto-delete when empty; manual clusters persist |
| **Execution log tracking** | YES for "done"/"resolve" actions, can log `cluster_name`, but no "cluster_done" event fires |
| **`desloppify next` + clusters** | Respects `active_cluster` focus, filters to cluster members, shows remaining count |
| **Queue representation** | Linear `queue_order[]` list; cluster membership tracked in `overrides` |

The system is designed to **prevent accidental bulk completion of small clusters** while **not automatically declaring a cluster "done"**—this requires explicit user awareness and action.
